TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于激活条件的选择性知识控制方法（activation-conditioned selective knowledge control），通过神经元级别的梯度操控实现GUI智能体的持续学习，在多个应用程序的序列化学习场景中有效缓解灾难性遗忘并保持对新应用的适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI智能体需要在不断演化的应用程序流中进行持续学习，既要利用新旧应用中共享的基础知识，又要保留各应用特有的知识而避免干扰历史记忆。现有的持续学习方法难以应对这种既需要共享又需要特异性的复杂知识建模问题。

Method: 该方法维护一个紧凑的历史知识状态以保护那些保留了先前知识的高激活MLP神经元。当新应用到来时，系统根据前向激活执行实时的梯度手术（gradient surgery）。具体而言，受保护的神经元被分为两类：未激活的神经元承载特定知识，其梯度被截断以防止干扰；激活的神经元承载共享知识，其梯度通过正交投影来保持稳定性同时允许适应。每个应用阶段结束后，新识别的关键神经元会被合并到历史状态中供未来学习使用。

Result: 在多个应用序列化学习的基准测试上的实证评估表明，该方法在有效缓解先前应用上的灾难性遗忘的同时，保持了对新应用的稳健适应能力。

Conclusion: 基于激活条件的神经元级梯度操控是一种轻量级的有效持续学习策略，能够在不显著增加计算开销的情况下，通过区分共享知识与特定知识来实现选择性知识保留，为GUI智能体在动态应用环境中的长期学习提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Continual learning is a crucial capability for Graphical User Interface (GUI) agents to adapt to evolving applications while retaining knowledge acquired from previous applications. Such application streams pose a challenging knowledge modeling problem: new applications often share underlying knowledge with past ones, yet also introduce specific knowledge that must not interfere with historical knowledge. In this paper, we propose activation-conditioned selective knowledge control, a lightweight method that achieves selective knowledge retention via neuron-level gradient manipulation. Our method maintains a compact historical knowledge state to protect highly activated MLP neurons that preserve previous knowledge. When a new application arrives, it performs real-time gradient surgery conditioned on forward activation. Concretely, the protected neurons are categorized into two types: unactivated neurons holding specific knowledge, whose gradients are truncated to prevent interference; and activated neurons holding shared knowledge, whose gradients are orthogonally projected to preserve stability while enabling adaptation. After each application stage, newly identified critical neurons are merged into the historical state for future learning. Empirical evaluations on multi-app sequential benchmark demonstrate that our method effectively mitigates catastrophic forgetting on prior applications while sustaining robust adaptation to new ones.

</details>


### [101] [3DHarnessBench: Probing Agentic 3D-to-Code Capabilities of Frontier Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06535)
*Ling Liu,Bingchen Gong,Amal Dev Parakkat,Maks Ovsjanikov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了3DHarnessBench，一个评估前沿视觉语言模型（VLM）将输入的3D几何信息恢复为Blender Python代码的智能体能力的基准测试框架。不同于以往固定输入的方法，该基准通过四种递进的harness设置（单视图、多视图、主动视觉和完整3D交互），利用Blender MCP功能赋予模型主动探索、工具调用和自我纠错的能力。实验发现所有前沿模型在更丰富的函数调用访问下3D重建能力显著提升，但这种提升高度依赖模型，揭示了模型间在agentic 3D到代码能力上的显著不均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM用于3D重建的方法通常给模型固定的输入（如单张渲染图或文本描述），限制了模型展示其主动探索、推理和工具调用能力。作者希望构建一个能系统评估VLM在agentic（智能体式）3D重建任务中能力的基准，特别是当模型被赋予渐进式增加的交互权限时，其3D几何恢复能力如何变化。

Method: 3DHarnessBench设计了四个递进层次的基准设置：(1) Single-view（单视图，仅给一张渲染图）；(2) Multi-view（多视图）；(3) Active Visual（任意视点访问，模型可主动选择视角）；(4) Full 3D Interaction（通过Blender函数调用获得目标物体的完整访问权限）。这些设置借助Blender MCP功能实现，允许模型进行主动的场景探索。评估维度涵盖视觉感知、主动推理、工具调用和自我纠错能力，要求模型输出Blender Python代码以恢复3D几何。

Result: 实验中观察到，所有前沿模型在获得更丰富的函数调用访问权限后，其恢复3D几何的能力均有显著提升。然而，这种提升幅度具有强烈的模型依赖性，显示出不同前沿VLM在agentic 3D-to-code能力上存在高度不均衡的现象。

Conclusion: 3DHarnessBench揭示了一个重要洞见：赋予VLM更丰富的交互和工具调用能力确实能提升其3D几何恢复表现，但模型间能力鸿沟显著。作者将公开基准测试、代码、输出和智能体轨迹，以支持可复现的3D agentic评估和后续研究。

Abstract: We introduce 3DHarnessBench, a benchmark that evaluates the agentic ability of frontier vision-language models (VLMs) to recover 3D geometry as Blender Python code from a variety of inputs. Unlike previous frameworks that prompt the VLMs with a fixed input (e.g., a single rendering or a text description), 3DHarnessBench evaluates four separate harness settings that progressively enable active agentic exploration, facilitated by recent Blender MCP functionality. Our hierarchy from Single-view, Multi-view, Active Visual (arbitrary viewpoint access), and Full 3D Interaction (complete access to the target object through Blender function calls) probes the models' abilities in both visual perception and active inference, tool calling, and self-correction. We observe that the ability of all frontier models to recover 3D geometry improves significantly with richer function call access, although the improvements are strongly model-dependent, revealing highly uneven agentic 3D-to-code capabilities. We will release the benchmark, code, outputs, and agent trajectories for reproducible 3D evaluation.

</details>


### [102] [Learning to Use Imagination: Progress-Conditioned Future Utilization for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06578)
*Yijie Zhu,Zitong Yu,Wei Li,Hui Ma,Wen Li,Rui Shao,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ProWAM（Progress-Conditioned World Action Model），通过显式的执行进度表征来自适应地利用想象未来，解决现有World Action Models中未来视觉预测在不同执行阶段和不同潜变量之间的非均匀效用问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有World Action Models（WAMs）将未来视觉动态融入动作生成，但想象未来缺乏对执行进度演化的自适应调整，可能引入干扰性或不可靠的预测线索。作者通过实证识别出两种未来效用的非均匀性：(i) 跨进度层面，未来想象的效用随执行阶段控制需求的变化而不同；(ii) 进度内层面，同一进度状态下不同未来潜变量的相关性存在异质性。

Method: ProWAM由两个紧耦合组件构成：(1) 自监督双时间进度编码器（SS-DTPE），耦合短期动作-观测交互建模与长期循环进度聚合，捕获近期执行反馈与累积任务历史，获得可靠的执行进度表征；(2) 层级进度条件化想象调制（HPIM），包含跨进度的全局调制机制（在阶段间调整未来利用方式）与进度内的相关性机制（区分同进度状态下各未来潜变量的相关性差异）。

Result: 在大量实验中，ProWAM在强VLA（Vision-Language-Action）和WAM基线上取得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过将执行进度作为显式中间表征并设计层级调制方法，ProWAM能有效缓解未来想象效用的非均匀性问题，从而在动作生成中更可靠地利用未来动态信息，超越现有基线的表现。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) extend Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models by incorporating future visual dynamics into action generation. However, existing WAMs often utilize imagined futures with limited adaptation to evolving execution progress, potentially introducing distracting or unreliable predictive cues. This limitation arises from two empirically identified forms of non-uniformity in future utility: (i) at the inter-progress level, the utility of imagined futures varies across execution stages as control demands change; and (ii) at the intra-progress level, individual future latents exhibit heterogeneous relevance within the same progress state. To address these limitations, we propose ProWAM, a Progress-Conditioned World Action Model that introduces execution progress as an explicit intermediate representation for adaptive imagination utilization. ProWAM comprises two tightly coupled components: (1) To obtain a reliable representation of execution progress, we propose the Self-Supervised Dual-Temporal Progress Encoder (SS-DTPE). SS-DTPE couples short-term action-observation interaction modeling with long-term recurrent progress aggregation to capture recent execution feedback and accumulated task history. (2) Conditioned on the progress representation from SS-DTPE, we propose the Hierarchical Progress-Conditioned Imagination Modulation (HPIM) to adapt imagination utilization to execution progress. HPIM operates at two complementary levels: an inter-progress global modulation mechanism adapts future utilization across execution stages, while an intra-progress relevance mechanism differentiates individual future latents within each progress state. Extensive experiments demonstrate consistent gains over strong VLA and WAM baselines.

</details>


### [103] [Reading Decoder Trajectories: Training-Free Counterfactual Query-Trajectory Reliability for Small-Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06581)
*Zhaoning Shi,Bo Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CQTR框架，通过反事实尺度干预激活冻结检测器中潜在的小目标知识，并结合解码器内部的轨迹可靠性评估，实现无需训练和无需目标域标注的小目标检测性能提升，在9个冻结检测器和3个数据集共27个组合上一致改进AP和APs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小目标检测面临像素少导致信息丢失的问题，现有方法假设冻结模型能力不足，通过多尺度训练、架构重设计或参数自适应来改进表示。作者挑战这一假设，认为小目标知识已存在于冻结检测器中，只是未被充分激活且在查询演化过程中不稳定，因此需要一种无需微调即可挖掘潜在尺度知识的机制。

Method: 提出CQTR（Counterfactual Query-Trajectory Reliability）框架：通过反事实尺度干预（counterfactual scale interventions）激活冻结模型的潜在响应；从解码器内部提取三类轨迹证据——空间收敛性（spatial convergence）、语义持久性（semantic persistence）和跨尺度冲突（cross-scale conflicts）来评估候选可靠性；利用少量无标注训练子集为每个模型-数据流选择适当的纠正机制，全程无需参数更新或目标域标注。

Result: 在9个冻结检测器与3个数据集的27种组合上，CQTR一致提升了平均精度（AP）和小目标平均精度（APs）。闭合循环分析表明尺度干预能激活潜在响应、轨迹证据可预测真实标注支持，无标注路由能选择更有效的分支。

Conclusion: CQTR将小目标检测的范式从外部尺度增强（external scale augmentation）重新定义为对潜在尺度知识的激活与可靠性评估（activation and reliability assessment），证明了冻结模型本身已蕴含小目标能力，无需任何训练即可系统性释放。

Abstract: Small-object detection remains challenging because limited pixels cause information loss and suppress the scale knowledge encoded in pretrained detectors. Existing approaches mainly improve representations through multiscale training, architecture redesign, or parameter adaptation, implicitly assuming that frozen models lack the required capability. We challenge this assumption and hypothesize that small-object knowledge already exists in frozen detectors but remains underactivated and unstable during query evolution. To test this hypothesis, we propose Counterfactual Query-Trajectory Reliability (CQTR), a training-free framework that elicits latent responses through counterfactual scale interventions and interprets candidate reliability from decoder-internal spatial convergence, semantic persistence, and cross-scale conflicts. A small unlabeled training subset selects the appropriate correction mechanism for each model-data stream, without parameter updates or target-domain annotations. Across 27 combinations of nine frozen detectors and three datasets, CQTR consistently improves average precision (AP) and average precision for small objects (APs). Closed-loop analyses further show that scale intervention activates latent responses, trajectory evidence predicts ground-truth support, and unlabeled routing selects the more effective branch. CQTR therefore reframes small-object detection from external scale augmentation to the activation and reliability assessment of latent scale knowledge.

</details>


### [104] [Multi-History-Step SDE Inversion for Image Editing with Superior Regional Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06602)
*Haiyan Wei,Yunlong Wang,Huaibo Huang,Zhenan Sun,Kunbo Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MIEdit，一个基于SDE反演的无训练图像编辑框架。通过引入预测-校正多历史步方案提升编辑质量并减少步数，缓解多条件噪声残差与梯度项的异质性和冲突，并提出Inversion-Time Automatic Semantic Angle Masking (IASM)利用无分类器引导自动生成语义角度掩码实现区域约束。另构建了包含30个细粒度任务、1000+图像-文本-掩码三元组的EditEval++基准，实验表明MIEdit优于现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散SDE反演和反演无关方法虽然无需训练即可实现忠实重建，但存在效率低下、可塑性有限以及难以精确保留未编辑区域的问题。

Method: 1) 提出预测-校正多历史步方案用于SDE反演；2) 缓解多条件噪声残差与梯度项在采样中的异质性和冲突，增强稳定性和编辑可塑性；3) 提出IASM模块，利用无分类器引导在反演阶段自动生成语义角度掩码并贯穿采样过程用于区域约束，无需额外用户输入；4) 构建EditEval++评测集。

Result: 在EditEval++（30个细粒度任务，1000+三元组）上的实验表明MIEdit在编辑质量和区域保持等方面优于现有最先进技术。

Conclusion: MIEdit通过创新的SDE反演方案、噪声/梯度冲突缓解机制以及自动语义掩码策略，实现了更高效、可塑性强且能精确保护未编辑区域的无训练图像编辑，并提供了更全面的评测基准。

Abstract: In recent years, diffusion stochastic differential equation (SDE) inversion and inversion-free methods have become prevalent for training-free image editing, as they can achieve faithful reconstruction without tuning. However, existing approaches remain inefficient, exhibit limited plasticity, and struggle to accurately preserve unedited regions. To address these issues, we propose MIEdit, a training-free editing framework based on SDE inversion. MIEdit introduces a predictor-corrector multi-history-step scheme to achieve superior editing quality with fewer steps. We further mitigate heterogeneity and conflict between the multi-conditioned noise residuals and gradient terms during sampling, improving stability and editing plasticity under large edits. MIEdit also includes Inversion-Time Automatic Semantic Angle Masking (IASM); it leverages classifier-free guidance to automatically generate semantic angle masks during inversion and applies them throughout the sampling process for regional constraints, without extra user inputs. We additionally construct EditEval++ (30 fine-grained tasks, 1,000+ image-text-mask triplets) for comprehensive evaluation; experiments show that MIEdit outperforms state-of-the-art techniques. Project page: https://whywwwzzzg.github.io/MIEdit/.

</details>


### [105] [GAN-Blot: A Controllable Structure-Style Synthesis Benchmark for Western Blot Forensics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06619)
*Hao-Chiang Shao,Fong-Yi Lin,Te-An Chien,TianYu Chen,Da-Jhong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GAN-Blot，一个可控的Western blot（WB）图像合成框架，通过将WB图像分解为结构组件和风格参考组件，实现局部蛋白条带几何形状和全局视觉外观的独立控制，生成高保真合成WB图像，用于推进WB图像取证技术的发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WB图像是生物医学研究中的关键证据，但近年来科研不端案例显示WB图像伪造现象日益严重，亟需有效的WB取证技术。然而WB取证技术的发展因缺乏标准化的外观属性定义、图像数据集和可控生成框架而受阻。

Method: GAN-Blot框架包括：1) 将WB图像分解为结构组件和风格参考组件的公式化表达；2) 双路径自编码设计结合多种风格对齐损失项，实现对结构-风格合成的隐式控制，无需预定义的语义外观属性；3) 构建包含46K+图像的合成WB数据集。

Result: 大量实验表明GAN-Blot能在蛋白条带结构和视觉风格两方面生成高保真WB图像。在盲测中，生成的图像能欺骗领域专家，现有检测器和筛查平台也无法可靠区分合成图像与真实WB图像。

Conclusion: GAN-Blot生成的图像可作为具有挑战性的受控案例，用于验证和开发WB取证方法，推动WB图像取证领域的发展。

Abstract: Western blot (WB) images are widely used as key evidence in biomedical research. Recent scientific misconduct cases reveal that WB imagery is increasingly fabricated, making WB forensics a major concern for research integrity. However, while the progress of forensic detection techniques often relies on advances in forgery-generation techniques, the development of WB forensic techniques has been hindered by the lack of standardized appearance attribute definitions, image datasets, and controllable generation frameworks for WB imagery. To address this limitation, we present a controllable WB image synthesis framework, named GAN-Blot, for generating realistic synthetic WB images. We introduce a formulation that decomposes a WB image into a structure component and a style-reference component, enabling independent control over local protein-band geometry and the global visual appearance of a synthetic WB image. GAN-Blot integrates a dual-path autoencoding design with several style-alignment loss terms to enable implicit control over structure-style synthesis without predefined semantic appearance attributes. We further contribute a synthetic WB dataset containing more than 46K images and propose four evaluation protocols for controllable WB synthesis. Extensive experiments show that GAN-Blot can generate WB images with high fidelity in both protein-band structure and visual style. Under blind inspection, the generated images can fool domain experts and are not reliably distinguished from authentic WB images by existing detectors and screening platforms. These results demonstrate their utility as challenging controlled cases for validating and developing WB forensic methods.

</details>


### [106] [GeoCo-SAVi: Geometry-Consistent Slot Attention for Explicitly Editable Object Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06628)
*Haoxiang Huang,Zhekai Wang,Xiang Liu,Sen Cui,Changshui Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoCo-SAVi是一种改进的对象中心视频模型，通过在ISA基础上引入几何权威性和语义对齐机制，使显式的位姿（位置和尺度）真正成为可控编辑指令。它在Obj3D和MOVi-C数据集上显著降低了质心误差和尺寸变化，同时保持了重建质量，实现了对场景几何的可读、可编辑的组成式控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对象中心视频模型（如ISA）虽然用slots表示场景，但显式的位姿（位置和尺度）与解码出的几何含义可能不一致——编辑位置或尺度会产生意外运动或尺寸变化，替换外观甚至会改变几何。这种"几何-外观"语义耦合问题使得显式几何无法真正作为可控的组成式编辑指令。

Method: GeoCo-SAVi提出三个关键机制：(1) 空间等变的对象级解码器，使位置和尺度成为有效的控制指令（改变它们即移动/缩放渲染支撑）；(2) 事实位置对齐将位置与解码中心绑定，归一化注意力重叠抑制重复分配；(3) 外观移植实现跨对象的几何语义对齐——受体对象几何决定布局，供体外观提供形状。此外采用时间初始化器在帧间传播校准后的slots。

Result: 在Obj3D数据集上与ISA重建质量持平，质心误差降低超过80%，外观诱导的尺寸变化减少超过50%，并能产生预期的平移和缩放响应。在250个MOVi-C视频上，相比两个同协议基线，改善了重建、实例分组和固定身份几何控制。

Conclusion: GeoCo-SAVi将显式几何转化为真正的组成式控制信号，使位置和尺度在编辑中更具可读性和可操作性，弥合了slot显式几何参数与实际解码内容之间的语义鸿沟。

Abstract: Object-centric video models represent scenes with slots, yet exposed geometry can vary in meaning with appearance. In Invariant Slot Attention (ISA), explicit position and scale can disagree with the decoded center and extent; edits can yield unexpected motion or resizing, and replacing appearance can shift geometry.
  GeoCo-SAVi promotes geometric authority and semantic alignment. Its spatially equivariant, object-wise decoder makes position and scale effective commands: changing them moves or resizes the rendered support. Factual position alignment ties position to the decoded center, and normalized attention overlap discourages duplicate allocation. Appearance transplantation aligns geometry semantics across objects, so recipient geometry governs layout while donor appearance supplies shape. A temporal initializer propagates calibrated slots across frames.
  On Obj3D, GeoCo-SAVi matches ISA reconstruction, reduces p-centroid error by over 80%, and cuts appearance-induced size variation by over 50% while producing the expected translation and scale responses. On 250 MOVi-C videos, it also improves reconstruction, instance grouping, and fixed-identity geometry control over two same-protocol references. GeoCo-SAVi transforms explicit geometry into compositional control, making both position and scale more readable and editable.

</details>


### [107] [Physico-Geospatial Grounded Scene Interpretation for Mobile Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06629)
*Nicolas Schuler,Janik Kurtz,Lea Dewald,Marcel Sauber,Félicia Teferle,Jürgen Graf*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种方法，通过将语义描述、OpenStreetMap建筑与街道数据、以及来自传感器的位置/时间/度量信息融合（利用LLM），增强预训练未修改的VLM在场景解释中的输出，使其具备物理-地理空间接地能力。在大学校园室外录制中，建筑接地F1为0.83，路面接地F1为0.64。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习使机器人能交互于动态非结构化环境，但将物理-地理空间世界知识整合进系统仍具挑战。作者希望增强VLM场景解释的地理空间接地能力，使其输出不仅依赖视觉特征，还能结合真实世界的物理与空间语义。

Method: 采用无需修改的预训练VLM进行场景解释，将其输出与语义描述、OpenStreetMap建筑数据与街道信息、以及从感官系统获得的位置、时间和度量信息结合，利用LLM对这些多源信息进行融合，生成物理-地理空间接地的自然语言描述。

Result: 在大学校园的室外录制环境中进行验证，建筑物接地任务F1分数为0.83，路径表面接地任务F1分数为0.64，展示了该方案的概念可行性。

Conclusion: 该方法在概念上能够生成具有物理-地理空间接地的自然语言场景描述，为机器人场景理解与物理世界知识融合提供了可行的技术路线。

Abstract: Recent advancements in deep learning allow robotic agents to interact with dynamic and unstructured environments. Of special interest is the integration of physico-geospatial world knowledge into such systems, either by using physics-aware machine learning models, knowledge graphs to model relationships or spatio-temporal and logical reasoning. In the present work, we introduce an approach to augment the output of pre-trained, unmodified VLMs used for scene interpretation by integrating semantic descriptions, OpenStreetMap building data and street information with positional, temporal and metric information obtained from our sensory systems, fusing this information using LLMs. We apply this concept to an outdoor recording within a university campus, achieving an F1-Score of 0.83 in the task of grounding buildings and 0.64 for path surface grounding on our pilot evaluation set. The results demonstrate the conceptual capability of the proposed solution to deliver physico-geospatial grounded natural language descriptions. Code and results are available at https://datahub.rz.rptu.de/hstr-csrl-public/publications/physico-geospatial-grounded-scene-interpretation

</details>


### [108] [MARR: Decoupling Policy, Execution, and Calibration for All-in-One Medical Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06645)
*Haobin Chen,Ao Chang,Heqin Zhu,Rundong Wang,Ting Liu,Shaohua Kevin Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MARR框架，将多模态医学图像复原中的有限适配问题分解为策略选择、特征执行和输出校准三个层面，通过退化感知的策略路由、模态私有残差执行和图像域残差校正，无需退化标签或独立模态模型即可完成PET/CT/MRI的统一复原，在基准上超越13种方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全共享网络会导致模态特有残差误差纠缠，而独立模态网络又牺牲了统一部署的实用性。PET、CT和MRI在退化统计、解剖对比度和输出空间偏差上差异显著，因此需要研究将有限适配能力放置何处（策略选择、特征执行或输出校准）以实现高效的全一体复原。

Method: MARR由三个核心组件构成：(1) 策略分支从输入统计、潜在内容和模态身份生成路由提示，仅作为控制信号使用；(2) 提示门控的模态私有适配器在中间解码器阶段执行轻量级残差细化；(3) 零初始化的模态特定输出头校准最终图像域残差，且不干扰初始共享预测。整个框架不需要退化标签或独立的模态特定模型。

Result: 在PET、CT和MRI全一体复原基准上，MARR在统一训练协议下超越13种方法，在PET、CT、MRI上分别达到37.34 dB、33.85 dB和32.09 dB的PSNR，模态平均PSNR为34.43 dB，为最优结果。

Conclusion: 将受限适配合理地分配到策略路由、私有残差执行和输出校准三个层面，可以在不牺牲精度的情况下实现多模态医学图像的高效统一复原，兼顾了性能与部署灵活性。

Abstract: All-in-one medical image restoration seeks to recover heterogeneous clinical images with a single model, but PET, CT, and MRI differ substantially in degradation statistics, anatomical contrast, and output-space bias. A fully shared network can entangle modality-specific residual errors, whereas separate modality-specific networks sacrifice the practical advantages of unified deployment. We therefore recast all-in-one restoration as a question of where limited adaptation should be placed: policy selection, feature execution, or output calibration. We propose MARR, a compact restoration framework that constrains multi-modality adaptation into degradation-aware policy routing, modality-private residual execution, and image-domain residual correction without requiring degradation labels or separate modality-specific models. The policy branch forms a routing prompt from input statistics, latent content, and modality identity, and uses it only as a control signal. Prompt-gated modality-private adapters then perform lightweight residual refinement at intermediate decoder stages, while zero-initialized modality-specific output heads calibrate the final image-domain residual without perturbing the initial shared prediction. On an all-in-one PET, CT, and MRI restoration benchmark, MARR outperforms thirteen methods re-trained under the same protocol, achieving PSNR values of 37.34 dB, 33.85 dB, and 32.09 dB on PET, CT, and MRI, respectively, and the best modality-average PSNR of 34.43 dB. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/CHB-learner/MARR.

</details>


### [109] [When Does a Laugh Begin? Structured Annotator Disagreement in Temporal Laughter Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06646)
*Eyal Hanania,Daniel Arkushin,Naveh Ayal,Jonathan Benvenisti,Amos Bercovich,Elie Zemmour,Sahar Froim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对笑声时间定位（temporal laughter localization）中标注者间分歧被忽视的问题，通过对SMILE-Temporal基准重新标注，揭示了结构化分歧模式（偏移处分歧大于起始处、轻笑声远多于大笑），并提出了一种基于一致性校准容忍带（conformally calibrated tolerance bands）的分歧校准评估方法，以替代单一参考标注的评估方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标注者在笑声边界和细微轻笑声上经常意见不一，但现有的时间笑声定位评测总使用单一参考标注进行评估。作者认为这种分歧是系统性的而非随机噪声，因此需要深入理解其结构并反哺评测方法。

Method: 1) 重新标注SMILE-Temporal基准（672个视频、1,683个事件），每个视频由3-5名标注者标注（alpha=0.757）；2) 分析分歧的结构模式（onset vs offset、chuckle vs laugh差异、事件属性预测性）；3) 提出分歧校准的评估方法：基于全标注者分布打分，使用一致性校准生成容忍带，容忍带在偏移处更宽（0.727秒）而起始处较窄（0.5秒）。

Result: 分歧在偏移处比起始处大1.73倍；轻笑声分歧远大于完整大笑（77% vs 20%）；分歧可由事件属性预测（AUC=0.831）；单一标注评测导致系统分数波动达0.246 F1，正确排序系统的成功率仅69.7%，而基于全标注者的评估可达80%。

Conclusion: 单一标注者评估在笑声时间定位任务上不可靠且会扭曲系统排名；应改用基于多标注者分布的分歧校准评估方法，并考虑时间边界处（尤其偏移）的不确定性，其标注数据和代码已开源。

Abstract: Annotators routinely disagree on laughter boundaries and subtle chuckles, yet temporal laughter localization typically evaluates against a single reference annotation. We show that this disagreement is structured rather than random noise. Re-annotating the SMILE-Temporal benchmark (672 videos, 1,683 events) with 3-5 annotators per video (alpha = 0.757), we find systematic patterns: disagreement is 1.73 times larger at offsets than onsets, far more common for chuckles than full laughs (77% vs. 20%), and predictable from event attributes (AUC = 0.831). Evaluating against a single annotator breaks down under this structure: system scores shift by 0.246 F1 depending on the chosen ground truth, correctly ranking systems only 69.7% of the time (vs. 80% against all annotators). We propose a disagreement-calibrated evaluation that scores predictions against the full annotator distribution using conformally calibrated tolerance bands (wider at offsets, 0.727s, than onsets, 0.5s). The per-annotator annotations and analysis code are available at https://github.com/WSCSports/SMILE-Disagreement .

</details>


### [110] [VidaForge: Open Research Infrastructure for Video Pretraining Data Recipes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06652)
*Yan Ma,Jiadi Su,Zhulin Hu,Ethan Chern,Linhao Zhang,Tiantian Mi,Pengfei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VIDAFORGE，一个开放的视频数据流水线研究基础设施，将视频数据配方表示为从原始视频到训练数据集的可执行五阶段工作流，并发布VIDAFORGE-3M数据集（314万个场景级片段，共6475小时），通过在Wan 2.1和V-JEPA 2.1上的早期预训练实验揭示数据配方选择与下游模型性能之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频基础模型依赖大规模预训练数据，但端到端数据流水线封闭且难以检查或复用。研究者需要构建大量基础设施才能验证一个聚焦的假设，缺乏开放、可执行的数据配方研究基础设施。

Method: 将视频数据配方形式化为从原始视频到训练数据集的可执行五阶段工作流（VIDAFORGE），该工作流中的决策可变化以构建替代数据集并保留每个样本的生产过程；在Wan 2.1和V-JEPA 2.1上比较不同覆盖度和质量的数据配方的早期from-scratch预训练实验；发布包含314万个场景级片段、共6475小时的VIDAFORGE-3M数据集，配有细粒度标注和策展信号。

Result: 在两种学习目标下，更广覆盖度的数据配方在下游基准测试中取得最高分数；而基于损失的评估则偏向于不同的配方选择。结果表明数据配方选择（覆盖度vs质量）在不同评估方式下存在权衡。

Conclusion: VIDAFORGE将数据配方选择与下游模型性能连接起来，为视频数据配方研究提供了开放的可执行基础设施与大规模标注数据集，验证了该工作流的实用性。

Abstract: Video foundation models increasingly rely on large-scale pretraining data, yet the end-to-end data pipelines behind them remain largely closed and difficult to inspect or reuse. Researchers seeking to understand how video data recipes affect model pretraining often need to build substantial infrastructure before testing even a focused hypothesis. We present VIDAFORGE, an open research infrastructure that represents a video data recipe as an executable five-stage workflow from raw videos to training datasets. A decision in this workflow can be varied to construct alternative datasets while preserving how every sample was produced. To demon strate this research workflow, we compare data recipes with different coverage and quality in early from-scratch pretraining of Wan 2.1 and V-JEPA 2.1. Across both learning objectives, the broader-coverage recipe achieves the highest downstream benchmark scores, while loss-based evaluation favors different recipes. This study demonstrates how VidaForge connects data-recipe choices to downstream model performance. We further release VIDAFORGE-3M, containing 3.14 million scene level clips totaling 6,475 hours, with fine-grained annotations and curation signals for video data-recipe research.

</details>


### [111] [FSAN: Flow State Attention Network for Aerodynamic Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06660)
*Wenxuan Jin,Jianguo Yao,Haibing Guan,Xijun Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出流态注意力网络（FSAN），通过可学习的软分配将几何划分为多个流态，实现几何与流动信息之间细粒度、状态特定的交互，在两个公开气动基准上超越最强基线，分别降低REL-L2误差超过20%（Emmi-Wing）和10%（DrivAerNet++）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CFD仿真计算昂贵且依赖专业知识，限制了其在迭代设计和实时分析中的应用。现有深度学习代理有两个局限：(i)仅在窄流动条件范围的数据集上评估，复杂流动条件下的表现未得到验证；(ii)将全局流动条件视为一个单一矢量统一注入所有表面点，忽略了不同几何区域经历不同局部流动现象，削弱了复杂条件下的预测精度。

Method: FSAN分别编码点云和流动条件，然后通过可学习的软分配将几何划分为多个流动状态，利用流动特征更新这些状态表示，状态变化进而影响点云特征，实现几何与流动信息间细粒度的状态特定交互。

Result: 在两个公开气动基准（Emmi-Wing和DrivAerNet++）上，FSAN在所有对比方法中取得最高精度，但计算成本更高。在Emmi-Wing上较最强基线Transolver降低REL-L2误差超过20%；在DrivAerNet++上较最强基线AdaField降低10%。

Conclusion: FSAN被证明是一种在具有多样化流动条件和几何形状的公开基准上具有前景的神经代理模型，能够在复杂流动条件下实现更精确的气动预测。

Abstract: Accurate aerodynamic prediction is critical for designing fuel-efficient and safe transportation systems such as aircraft and automobiles, yet traditional computational fluid dynamics (CFD) simulations remain computationally expensive and expertise-intensive, severely limiting their use in iterative design and real-time analysis. Existing deep learning surrogates suffer from two major limitations: (i) they are evaluated on datasets with narrow flow-condition ranges, leaving their performance under complex flow conditions undemonstrated; (ii) they treat global flow conditions as a single vector injected uniformly across all surface points, ignoring that different geometric regions experience distinct local flow phenomena, which degrades prediction accuracy under complex flow conditions. To address these limitations, we propose the Flow State Attention Network (FSAN). FSAN separately encodes point cloud and flow conditions, then partitions the geometry into multiple flow states via learnable soft assignments, and uses flow features to update these state representations, which in turn influence point cloud features through state changes. This enables fine-grained, state-specific interaction between geometry and flow information. Extensive experiments on two well-recognized aerodynamic benchmarks demonstrate that FSAN achieves the highest accuracy among the methods compared in this work at a higher computational cost. On Emmi-Wing, FSAN reduces the Relative L2 (REL-L2) error by over 20\% compared to the strongest baseline (Transolver), and on DrivAerNet++, it achieves a 10\% reduction compared to the strongest baseline (AdaField). These results establish FSAN as a promising neural surrogate on public benchmarks with diverse flow conditions and geometries.

</details>


### [112] [TransNormal-2: Geometry-Grounded Rectified Flow with Edge-Aware Decoding for Precise Normal Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06665)
*Mingwei Li,Yi Yang,Hehe Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TransNormal-2是一个基于FLUX.2的rectified-flow单目法线估计框架，针对VAE重建退化导致的边界像素级精度问题，通过几何感知像素空间损失（含逆渲染自一致性、von Mises-Fisher角度损失和小波边缘感知正则化）与轻量级几何精化模块（GRM）在训练和推理两端同时修正解码误差。在通用场景基准上以更少的任务标注匹配或超越MoGe-2，在透明物体场景上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散式单目几何估计模型的像素级精度受限于VAE重建退化——8倍空间压缩导致物体表面法线在边界处劣化，即使对真值法线进行编解码也引入1.3-8.5°的平均角度误差，且边缘MAE高达全局MAE的2.8倍。现有方法忽视了这一共有的误差来源，缺乏从VAE解码器两侧系统性解决退化的方案。

Method: 在VAE解码器的两侧分别处理退化问题：(1) 训练侧采用几何感知像素空间损失——包括逆渲染自一致性损失、von Mises-Fisher角度损失和小波边缘感知正则化，与潜在空间MSE互补，在VAE解码后强化球面法线几何属性和漫反射图像形成约束；(2) 推理侧引入轻量级几何精化模块（GRM），利用RGB引导对粗预测施加残差修正，减少边界局部解码误差而不自由改写粗预测。整体基于FLUX.2的rectified-flow框架实现单步确定性推理。

Result: 在通用场景基准上，TransNormal-2在全部八项指标上匹配或超越MoGe-2，同时仅使用后者1.4%的任务特定法线标注量。在透明物体任务上优势最为显著，ClearGrasp上MAE降低4.2°，ClearPose上MAE降低3.1°，优于最强先验基线。

Conclusion: TransNormal-2通过系统性地针对VAE重建退化进行训练损失设计和推理端残差校正，有效提升了单目法线估计的像素级边界精度，并在极小标注预算下达到业界领先水平，尤其适用于透明物体等边界面挑战场景。

Abstract: Diffusion-based models enable monocular geometry estimation, yet their pixel-space precision is limited by a shared, under-studied error source: VAE reconstruction degradation. The 8x spatial compression in the VAE encoder-decoder degrades surface normals at object boundaries; even encoding and decoding ground-truth normals introduces 1.3--8.5° of mean angular error (MAE), with edge MAE reaching 2.8x the global MAE. We present TransNormal-2, a FLUX.2-based rectified-flow framework with single-step deterministic inference that addresses this degradation on both sides of the VAE decoder: in how latent predictions are supervised during training, and in how decoded normals are corrected at inference. First, geometry-aware pixel-space losses, including inverse rendering self-consistency, von~Mises-Fisher angular loss, and wavelet edge-aware regularization, complement latent MSE by enforcing spherical normal geometry and diffuse image-formation cues after VAE decoding. Second, a lightweight Geometric Refinement Module (GRM) applies an RGB-guided residual correction to reduce boundary-localized decoding errors without freely rewriting the coarse prediction. On general-scene benchmarks, TransNormal-2 matches or exceeds MoGe-2 on all eight reported metrics while using only 1.4% as many task-specific normal annotations. The gains are clearest for transparent objects, reducing MAE by 4.2° on ClearGrasp and 3.1° on ClearPose over the strongest prior baselines. Code will be released at https://longxiang-ai.github.io/TransNormal-2.

</details>


### [113] [Attention-Enhanced Deep Features with Heterogeneous Ensemble Learning for Glaucoma Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06699)
*Abdullah Al Shafi,Nishat Sadaf Lira,Abrar Hasan,Kazi Saeed Alam,Swapnil Kundu Argha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种结合注意力增强深度特征提取与异构集成学习的青光眼混合检测框架。利用InceptionV3结合CBAM提取判别性视网膜特征，通过SMOTE+Tomek Links解决类别不平衡，并采用单层集成(SLE)和双层集成(DLE)策略增强分类鲁棒性。在两个公开视网膜眼底数据集上验证了该方法的优越性能，并通过Grad-CAM可视化提供了可解释性证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 青光眼是进行性视神经病变，及时诊断至关重要。现有深度学习方法存在三个局限：(1)忽视特征精炼；(2)面临类别不平衡问题；(3)依赖单一分类器导致预测鲁棒性受限。

Method: (1)使用InceptionV3提取深度特征；(2)引入CBAM（卷积块注意力模块）精炼特征以增强判别性视网膜特征；(3)采用SMOTE+Tomek Links组合方法缓解类别不平衡；(4)使用多种机器学习模型配合单层集成(SLE)和双层集成(DLE)策略进行分类；(5)系统比较手工特征、深度特征和注意力增强深度特征三种表示方法。

Result: 在两个公开视网膜眼底数据集上的实验表明：基于深度特征的方法一致优于手工特征方法，所提出的注意力增强框架取得最佳整体性能。Grad-CAM可视化验证模型聚焦于临床相关的视网膜区域。

Conclusion: 注意力增强的深度特征与异构集成学习的组合能够有效提升青光眼检测性能，同时Grad-CAM提供了模型预测过程的可解释性证据，验证了模型关注的区域具有临床相关性。

Abstract: Glaucoma is a progressive optic neuropathy characterized by irreversible damage to the optic nerve, making timely diagnosis critical to prevent permanent vision loss. Although deep learning has demonstrated promising performance in automated glaucoma detection, existing approaches often overlook feature refinement, suffer from class imbalance, and rely on individual classifiers that limit prediction robustness. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a hybrid glaucoma detection framework that integrates attention-enhanced deep feature extraction with heterogeneous ensemble learning. Specifically, deep representations are extracted using InceptionV3 and subsequently refined by incorporating the Convolutional Block Attention Module (CBAM) to enhance discriminative retinal features. To improve classification robustness, the extracted features are classified using multiple machine learning models together with Single-Level Ensemble (SLE) and Double-Level Ensemble (DLE) strategies, while SMOTE combined with Tomek Links (SMOTE+TL) is employed to alleviate class imbalance. Furthermore, a systematic comparison of handcrafted, deep, and attention-enhanced deep feature representations is conducted. Experimental evaluation on two public retinal fundus datasets demonstrates that deep feature-based methods consistently outperform handcrafted feature-based methods, while the proposed attention-enhanced framework achieves the best overall performance. Furthermore, Grad-CAM visualizations confirm that the proposed model focuses on clinically relevant retinal regions, providing interpretable evidence on the model's prediction process.

</details>


### [114] [Counterfactual Tests for Measuring Chain-of-Thought Faithfulness in Visual Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06704)
*Bayar Menzat,Maximilian Süss,Ruizhi Wang,Benno Steinegger,Thomas Lukasiewicz,Oana-Maria Camburu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次将反事实测试（CT）和相关性反事实测试（CCT）方法适配到视觉输入，提出vCT和vCCT来衡量视觉语言模型（VLMs）思维链（CoT）的忠实性。实验在8个开源VLM上、两个数据集上进行，发现CoT并不能可靠地追踪影响模型预测的视觉证据，而Predict-then-Explain解释比回答前CoT与扰动引发的概率变化更一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对文本输入的CoT忠实性测量方法已日益增多，但将这些方法直接应用于视觉输入并不直接适用。需要一种能衡量VLMs思维链是否忠实反映其决策过程的方法。

Method: 将反事实测试（CT）和相关性反事实测试（CCT）适配到视觉输入，提出vCT和vCCT。在8个开源VLMs上，利用两个数据集（Counter-SNLI-VE和Counter-A-OKVQA，由单对象差异的图像对构成）进行基准测试，并引入重建控制实验排除编辑管线本身的影响。

Result: 研究发现：(1) CoT不能可靠地追踪影响模型预测的视觉证据——对象被移除导致大预测变化时CoT可能不提及，预测变化小时却可能被提及；(2) Predict-then-Explain解释与扰动引发的概率变化的一致性优于回答前CoT；(3) 二元vCT分数往往接近饱和；(4) 重建控制显示对象移除干预造成的模型变化大于单纯重建。

Conclusion: 视觉语言模型的思维链往往看似合理但未必忠实反映其决策依据；反事实方法适配到视觉输入后可用于系统评估VLM思维链的忠实性，且Predict-then-Explain模式比回答前CoT更可靠。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) may often look plausible, yet it may not faithfully reflect the model's decision-making process. While methods for measuring the faithfulness of CoTs for textual inputs have been increasingly introduced, using these methods for visual inputs is not straightforward. In this work, we adapt the family of counterfactual methods for measuring CoT faithfulness, namely the Counterfactual Test (CT) and Correlational Counterfactual Test (CCT), to visual inputs, and call them vCT and vCCT, respectively. Using vCT and vCCT, we benchmark eight recent open-source Vision Language Models (VLMs) on two datasets. Our analysis shows that CoTs do not reliably track visual evidence that influences model predictions: they may omit the removed object even when its removal causes a large prediction shift, yet mention it when the shift is small. We further find that Predict-then-Explain explanations align more strongly with perturbation-induced probability shifts than pre-answer CoTs, while binary vCT scores are often nearly saturated. We also include a reconstruction control, in which images pass through the same editing pipeline without object removal, and find that the main object-removal intervention induces larger shifts than reconstruction alone. We construct and release Counter-SNLI-VE and Counter-A-OKVQA, two datasets of image pairs that differ by a single object.

</details>


### [115] [RoLA: Rotary-Positioned Low-Rank Linear Attention for Efficient Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06712)
*Zekun Zhang,Yixiang Cai,Yuxi Liu,Tengxu Sun,Tianle Liu,Zhoutong Wu,Haoyu Li,Baole Ai,Ang Wang,Jiamang Wang,Lin Qu,Kun Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RoLA，一种基于旋转位置编码的线性注意力分支，用于视频扩散Transformer的稀疏-低秩混合架构中，在不增加额外位置参数的前提下保持真正的跨token全局聚合与相对旋转几何特性，在90%稀疏度下保持生成质量并实现2.63倍端到端推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散Transformer的密集时空自注意力随序列长度呈二次方增长，成为主要推理瓶颈。现有稀疏低秩混合方法中，全局分支在3D RoPE下存在结构兼容性问题：RoPE旋转操作与非线性特征映射一般不交换，导致难以在保持相对旋转几何的同时维持查询无关的线性摘要。现有规避方案（如坐标条件替代或学习绝对位置模块）本质上是从绝对坐标近似相对衰减，且引入额外位置参数。

Method: RoLA将RoPE放置在非线性低秩特征映射之外，并复用预训练旋转计划中的截断子集以匹配低秩瓶颈。这使得全局分支实现线性时间的低秩注意力，天然具备相对位置行为且无需额外位置参数。整体稀疏-低秩模块仍包含固定稀疏性的稀疏分支，形成完整的混合架构。

Result: 在开源视频DiT上的实验表明，该方法在90%稀疏度下保持具有竞争力的生成质量，在Wan2.1-14B（720p、81帧、NVIDIA H100 GPU）上实现2.63倍端到端推理加速。

Conclusion: RoLA通过将RoPE应用到非线性低秩映射之外并截断复用预训练旋转计划，解决了视频DiT稀疏低秩混合架构中全局分支的旋转-非线性不交换问题，在不引入额外位置参数的情况下实现了线性时间、具有相对位置行为且保持生成质量的高效全局注意力分支。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) achieve strong video generation quality, but their dense spatiotemporal self-attention scales quadratically with sequence length and quickly becomes the dominant inference bottleneck. Sparse low-rank hybrids alleviate this cost by combining a local sparse branch with a global compressed branch. In video DiTs equipped with 3D Rotary Position Embeddings (RoPE), the global branch faces a structural compatibility issue: when RoPE is applied before a nonlinear feature map, the rotation and nonlinearity generally do not commute, making it difficult to keep a query-independent linear summary while preserving relative rotary geometry. Existing work often sidesteps this issue by replacing genuine cross-token global aggregation with coordinate-conditioned surrogates or learnable absolute positional modules. These compromises can be effective, but they approximate relative decay from absolute coordinates and introduce extra positional parameters. We propose \textbf{RoLA}, a rotary-positioned low-rank linear-attention branch that keeps genuine cross-token aggregation while remaining compatible with a reusable linear summary. The design applies RoPE \emph{outside} the nonlinear low-rank feature map and reuses a truncated subset of the pre-trained rotary schedule matched to the low-rank bottleneck.
  This yields a linear-time low-rank global branch with relative positional behavior by design and no additional positional parameters; the full sparse--low-rank module still includes the fixed-sparsity sparse branch. Experiments on open-source video DiTs show that the resulting method remains competitive in generation quality at 90\% sparsity while achieving 2.63$\times$ end-to-end inference speedup on Wan2.1-14B (720p, 81 frames, measured on an NVIDIA H100 GPU).

</details>


### [116] [Companion-style QA Assistance in Ego-Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06721)
*Hangyu Qin,Junbin Xiao,Shenglang Zhang,Angela Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BuddyVQA基准，专注于日常第一人称（egocentric）流式视频中的“伴侣式问答”，包含21.6K问题、6K高亮时刻和1,012个长视频。该基准强调两个被现有VideoQA基准忽视的关键特性：自我指示词（ego-deictic expressions）和交互式链式问题。作者提出MyBuddy问答助手，利用多模态链式思维推理机制结合问题过滤器和多级记忆，显著提升基础模型在BuddyVQA及其他流式/通用视频QA基准上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VideoQA基准大多忽略日常第一人称问答辅助中的两个重要特性：(1) ego-deictic表达（涉及“这个/那个/它”等视觉代词，需结合第一人称视角理解用户意图）；(2) 交互式链式问题（前一个问题与后一个问题相互关联，如“在哪里？”→“怎么到达那里？”）。此外，现有工作未充分考虑长视频流式场景中的历史QA与视觉内容获取问题，需要新的基准与模型来解决这些挑战。

Method: (1) 构建BuddyVQA基准：基于1,012个长时第一人称视频，标注21.6K个问题与6K个高亮时刻，覆盖ego-deictic表达和交互式链式问题。(2) 提出MyBuddy模型：采用多模态链式思维（multimodal chain-of-thought）推理机制，基于历史QA与视觉内容推断最终答案；设计了问题过滤器（question filter）筛选相关信息，以及多级记忆（multi-level memory）机制在流式问答设置下高效进行QA与视觉信息检索。

Result: 实验表明MyBuddy在BuddyVQA上显著提升基础模型（foundation models）的性能；同时该方法的优势可泛化到其他流式视频和通用视频QA基准，验证了其适用性与有效性。代码和数据集已开源。

Conclusion: BuddyVQA填补了第一人称伴侣式视频问答基准的空白，MyBuddy通过多模态链式推理与高效检索机制为流式视频QA提供了有效方案。未来工作可在该基准上进一步探索更强的推理与记忆策略，推动AI伴侣在实际日常场景中的应用。

Abstract: AI companions are envisioned as always-on assistants that support users in daily life. With this regard, we introduce BuddyVQA, a benchmark for companion-style question answering (QA) on egocentric streaming video. BuddyVQA contains 21.6K questions linked to 6K highlight moments across 1,012 long, egocentric videos. It features two key characteristics that are common in daily first-person QA assistance but are largely overlooked in existing VideoQA benchmarks: ego-deictic expressions and interactively chained questions (e.g., "Where is it?", "How to get there?"). These require models to infer a user's in-situation intent by resolving visual pronouns in the context of egocentric visual and QA contents, with both grounded in a long-form streaming setting. To tackle the challenges, we propose MyBuddy, a companion-style QA assistant that highlights a multimodal chain-of-thought reasoning mechanism to infer the final answer based on the historical QA and visual content. An additional question filter and multi-level memory are designed to facilitate efficient QA and visual information retrieval under streaming QA settings. Experiments show that MyBuddy significantly enhances the performance of foundation models on BuddyVQA. Moreover, these gains generalize to other streaming and common video QA benchmarks, demonstrating the applicability and effectiveness of our approach. Our code and dataset are available at https://github.com/QHUni/BuddyVQA

</details>


### [117] [ADELE - Adaptive Delaunay Grids for High-Fidelity Mesh-Native Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06723)
*Johannes Weidenfeller,Shaofei Wang,Philipp Fürnstahl,Siyu Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应的网格优化框架，结合可优化的Delaunay四面体网格与多分辨率哈希网格，通过体积渲染引导粗几何、网格渲染恢复细节，并引入可微的深度偏移光栅化渲染公式，在不依赖NeRF/3DGS后处理的情况下直接优化高质量网格，在多种物体中心基准上显著超越现有网格优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高质量网格重建方法大多先学中间表示（NeRF/3DGS）再做网格提取，导致表面过度平滑或网格质量差、三角形数量过多；而网格原生优化方法受限于固定分辨率离散化和不稳定的优化行为。作者旨在直接从网格角度出发，兼顾粗几何引导与细粒度细节恢复，实现高质量网格的端到端优化。

Method: 该方法将可优化的Delaunay三角化四面体网格与多分辨率哈希网格相结合：前者通过点剪枝与插入进行自适应细化，后者提供SDF/外观预测所需的潜特征。优化流程先用体积渲染引导粗几何生成，再通过网格渲染恢复细粒度细节。此外提出一种可微的、基于光栅化的深度偏移渲染公式，以减少几何伪影并提升重建质量。

Result: 该方法在多种物体中心基准上显著优于现有网格优化方法，并与最先进的NeRF/3DGS方法性能相当。

Conclusion: 自适应四面体网格结合哈希特征表示，配合体积渲染与网格渲染的两阶段策略及深度偏移光栅化，能够在不依赖传统后处理网格提取的情况下直接生成高质量网格，兼顾了重建质量与实用性。

Abstract: Meshes remain the most practical representation for geometry reasoning and integration into graphics pipelines, yet existing reconstruction methods struggle to produce high-quality meshes. Most state-of-the-art approaches initially learn an intermediate representation (NeRF/3DGS) and treat mesh extraction as a post-processing step, which often leads to oversmoothed surfaces or poor quality meshes with excessive triangle counts.Existing mesh-native optimization methods alleviate some of these issues but suffer from fixed-resolution discretizations and unstable optimization behavior. In this paper, we introduce an adaptive mesh-based optimization framework and a practical mesh rendering technique to address these challenges. Our representation combines an optimizable Delaunay-triangulated tetrahedral grid with a multi-resolution hash grid. The former is refined through point pruning and insertion, while the latter provides latent features for SDF/appearance value predictions. We use volumetric rendering to bootstrap a coarse geometry while leveraging mesh-based rendering for recovering fine-grained details. Additionally, we propose a differentiable, rasterization-based depth-offset rendering formulation, reducing geometric artifacts and improving reconstruction quality. Our method significantly outperforms existing mesh optimization approaches across a variety of object-centric benchmarks while being competitive with state-of-the-art NeRF/3DGS methods.

</details>


### [118] [Back to the Feature: Zero-Shot 6DoF Pose Estimation via Dense Local Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06726)
*Ali Rafiaei,Michael Greenspan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出B2TFPose，一种无需训练的零样本6DoF物体姿态估计方法，利用单个冻结的DINOv3视觉Transformer提取跨合成到真实域泛化的稠密patch级特征，并通过三种创新策略（测地线非极大值抑制的模板选取、渲染引导的重新对应精化、多掩码假设选择）在BOP Benchmark七个核心数据集上实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有6DoF姿态估计方法依赖任务特定的训练数据和微调，难以泛化到未见物体。经典局部特征匹配范式虽优雅，但手工特征在合成到真实的域差距下缺乏鲁棒性。作者希望利用大规模自监督基础模型的强大泛化能力，在不进行任何训练/微调的前提下实现零样本姿态估计，从而突破数据驱动的局限。

Method: B2TFPose仅使用一个冻结的DINOv3作为唯一预训练组件。具体包含三大贡献：(1) 测地线非极大值抑制（geodesic NMS）从模板库中检索视角多样化的模板子集，用于粗到细的对应匹配；(2) 渲染引导的重新对应（RRC）机制，在估计姿态处合成物体特定视图并重新建立稠密2D-3D对应关系，在无额外可学习参数的情况下锐化初始估计；(3) 多掩码假设选择策略，联合评分多个竞争的分割候选以解决分割模糊性问题。

Result: 在BOP Benchmark的七个核心数据集上，B2TFPose在无精化时达到40.7的平均AR，有精化时达到56.4，在无需训练的RGB方法中确立SOTA性能，并超越了包括GigaPose和GenFlow在内的有训练方法，且推理速度具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了大规模自监督基础模型可以复兴传统局部特征匹配范式，以训练自由的方式实现与甚至超越有监督方法的6DoF姿态估计性能，展现了零样本泛化的巨大潜力。

Abstract: We present B2TFPose, a training-free zero-shot method for 6DoF pose estimation of unseen objects from RGB images. Using a single frozen DINOv3 vision transformer as its only pretrained component within the pose estimation pipeline, B2TFPose extracts dense patch-level features that generalize across the synthetic-to-real domain gap without any task-specific fine-tuning, revisiting the classical local feature matching paradigm through the lens of large-scale self-supervised foundation models. Three contributions advance the training-free state of the art. A geodesic non-maximum suppression strategy retrieves a viewpoint-diverse template set for coarse-to-fine correspondence matching. Render-guided Re-Correspondence (RRC) synthesizes object-specific views at the estimated pose and re-establishes dense 2D-3D correspondences to sharpen the initial estimate without additional learned parameters. A multi-mask hypothesis selection strategy jointly scores competing segmentation candidates to resolve segmentation ambiguity. On the seven core datasets of the BOP Benchmark, B2TFPose achieves 40.7 mean AR without refinement and 56.4 with refinement, establishing state-of-the-art performance among training-free RGB methods and outperforming trained counterparts including GigaPose and GenFlow, at competitive inference speed.

</details>


### [119] [Uni-Light: An Ultra-Lightweight Framework via Uncertainty-Aware Knowledge Distillation for Brain Tumour Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06729)
*Libing Kuang,Soren Salehi,Ziling Wu,Ahmad P. Tafti,Armaghan Moemeni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Uni-Light，一种超轻量级3D脑肿瘤分割框架，通过多尺度卷积、不确定性感知知识蒸馏和Signed Distance Field边界损失，在BraTS2023-GLI和MSD-BTS数据集上实现了参数减少97.56%、FLOPs减少73.03%、推理内存占用减少81.58%，同时Dice分数较SOTA平均提升1.47%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脑肿瘤分割方法计算开销大，而轻量级架构又难以在复杂肿瘤区域保持分割精度。需要在计算效率与分割保真度之间取得更优平衡，以满足资源受限临床场景的需求。

Method: 提出Uni-Light框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 多尺度卷积模块用于捕获不同尺度的肿瘤特征；(2) 不确定性感知知识蒸馏机制，利用教师模型的不确定性信息引导学生模型关注难以分类的区域；(3) Signed Distance Field边界损失提供几何约束，增强边界分割精度。

Result: 在BraTS2023-GLI和MSD-BTS两个数据集上验证：参数减少97.56%，FLOPs降低73.03%，推理内存占用减少81.58%，Dice分数较SOTA平均提升1.47%，实现了精度与效率的优越权衡。

Conclusion: Uni-Light展示了在资源受限的临床环境中进行高保真脑肿瘤分割的可行性。此外，该工作表明教师模型的预测不确定性可以作为数据驱动的监督信号，在不增加额外标注的前提下重新优先化训练数据分布，为医学影像数据工程提供了新思路。

Abstract: Accurate 3D brain tumour segmentation from multi-modal Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI) is essential for clinical diagnosis and treatment planning. Existing brain tumour segmentation methods often suffer from heavy computational demands, while current lightweight architectures frequently lack the capacity to maintain segmentation fidelity in complex tumour regions. To address these issues, we propose a novel ultra-lightweight framework (Uni-Light) that achieves high-fidelity segmentation with substantially reduced computational overhead. It combines multi-scale convolutions with an uncertainty-aware knowledge distillation scheme that directs the student model toward hard-to-classify regions, complemented by a Signed Distance Field boundary loss for geometric constraints. Experimental results on BraTS2023-GLI and MSD-BTS datasets demonstrate that Uni-Light reduces parameters by 97.56%, floating-point operations (FLOPs) by 73.03%, and inference memory footprint by 81.58%, while surpassing the state-of-the-art model by an average of 1.47% in Dice score, offering a highly competitive trade-off between segmentation accuracy and computational efficiency in resource-constrained clinical settings. This work also advances data engineering for medical imaging by demonstrating that teacher model uncertainty can be exploited as a data-driven supervisory signal, re-prioritising the training data distribution without requiring additional annotation.

</details>


### [120] [LASSNet: Level-Aware Availability-Conditioned Spatial-Semantic Fusion for Brain Tumor Segmentation with Missing MRI Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06733)
*Haobin Chen,Ao Chang,Rundong Wang,Zhicheng Li,Zhihao Tang,Heqin Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LASSNet，一种层级感知、可用性条件化的空间-语义融合网络，用于多模态MRI脑肿瘤分割中部分模态缺失的场景。网络包含两个层级特异模块：HACF处理高分辨率横向特征的可用性条件融合，TriRSF建模瓶颈级的模态间关系与空间上下文，通过粗到细解码器逐级注入特征，在所有15种非空模态配置上于BraTS2019和BraTS2023分别达到76.7%和83.2%的平均Dice。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态MRI脑肿瘤分割依赖四个成像序列的互补信息，但实际中因采集成本、协议变化、扫描失败或患者状况，一个或多个模态可能缺失。现有方法研究了重建、知识迁移和直接特征融合，但未探讨缺失模态融合是否应随表示层级变化。高分辨率横向特征保留空间细节，压缩瓶颈特征编码语义和模态间上下文，因此融合应同时受模态可用性和特征层级条件的联合制约。

Method: 提出LASSNet（Level-Aware Availability-Conditioned Spatial-Semantic Fusion Network），包含两个层级特异模块：(1) HACF（Hierarchical Availability-Conditioned Fusion）利用可用模态的计数归一化聚合、掩码条件通道调制和局部3D细化构建四个横向表示；(2) TriRSF（Tri-Scale Relational-Spatial Fusion）在多个瓶颈分辨率上建模可用模态描述符之间的关系与空间上下文，进行跨尺度聚合和可用性条件化的全局空间注意力。共享的粗到细解码器从TriRSF语义出发，逐步注入HACF特征，无需重建缺失输入。

Result: 在所有15种非空模态配置上，LASSNet在BraTS2019和BraTS2023上分别获得WT、TC、ET三类肿瘤区域的平均Dice分数76.7%和83.2%。

Conclusion: 验证了融合策略应依特征层级和模态可用性联合条件化的假设：高分辨率特征侧重空间细节融合，瓶颈特征侧重语义与模态间关系融合，所提出的层级感知融合框架在缺失模态场景下取得了优异的脑肿瘤分割性能。

Abstract: Brain tumor segmentation from multimodal MRI relies on complementary evidence across four imaging sequences, yet one or more modalities may be unavailable because of acquisition cost, protocol variation, scan failure, or patient condition. Existing work has explored reconstruction, knowledge transfer, and direct feature fusion, but leaves open whether missing-modality fusion should change with representation level. High-resolution lateral features retain spatial detail, whereas compressed bottleneck features encode semantic and inter-modality context. We therefore hypothesize that fusion should be conditioned jointly on modality availability and feature hierarchy. We propose the Level-Aware Availability-Conditioned Spatial-Semantic Fusion Network (LASSNet), which contains two level-specialized modules. Hierarchical Availability-Conditioned Fusion (HACF) constructs four lateral representations using count-normalized aggregation of available modalities, mask-conditioned channel modulation, and local 3D refinement. Tri-Scale Relational-Spatial Fusion (TriRSF) models relations among available modality descriptors and spatial context across multiple bottleneck resolutions, followed by cross-scale aggregation and availability-conditioned global spatial attention. A shared coarse-to-fine decoder starts from TriRSF semantics and progressively injects HACF features, without reconstructing missing inputs. Across all 15 non-empty modality configurations, LASSNet obtains mean Dice scores of 76.7% and 83.2% over WT, TC, and ET on BraTS2019 and BraTS2023, respectively.

</details>


### [121] [Agentic Visual Generation: From Generative Models to Agentic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06758)
*Yinming Huang,Shuyuan Tu,Xi Yan,Jiahao Zhan,Zihan Yang,Zhen Xing,Hui Zhang,Tiehua Zhang,Yu-Gang Jiang,Zuxuan Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种根据控制器在生成过程中可做出的决策范围来划分视觉生成系统智能体等级（L0至L4）的分类框架，替代以往基于工具使用、规划深度等非决定性标准的划分方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉生成系统从单次调用生成模型演变为智能体化控制流程，但缺乏一致的标准来判断一个生成系统何时变得具有智能体性。规划深度、工具使用、多角色协作和强化学习等常被视为智能体性的证据，但这些并不一定能决定控制器在生成过程中可做出的决策范围。

Method: 根据控制器在生成流程中能直接控制的内容来组织分类：L1（条件控制）仅准备输入；L2（执行控制）选择并调用实际的生成、编辑、渲染等操作；L3（结果自适应控制）观察中间结果并据此改变当前任务中的后续操作；L4（经验自适应控制）从已完成任务中保留经验并用于改变未来任务的决策；L0（固定支持）指无部署控制器的生成器、编辑器、评估器、奖励模型等固定流水线。该框架关注的维度是控制器决策范围的逐步扩展，而非模型大小、系统复杂度或训练方式。

Result: 将该框架应用于图像、视频、编辑、3D、世界、幻灯片和用户界面生成等多个领域，揭示了控制器能力的演变过程以及其机制在各等级间的分布情况。

Conclusion: 该分类框架（L0-L4）提供了一种更本质的视角来衡量视觉生成系统的智能体化程度，核心判据是控制器所能做出的生成级决策范围，这一标准独立于实现方式、规模或复杂度指标。

Abstract: Visual generation is evolving from generative models used through a single invocation into agentic control processes that can plan, select tools, inspect intermediate synthesized outputs, revise failures, and reuse prior experience. In most existing systems, the controller is an LLM or VLM, while visual generation models serve as tools or executors. However, existing work lacks a consistent criterion for determining when a generation system becomes agentic. Planning depth, tool use, multi-role collaboration, and reinforcement learning are often treated as evidence of agenticity, even though none of them necessarily determines which generation decisions the controller can make. We organize the field according to what the controller can directly control in the generation process. At L1 Conditioning Control, the controller prepares the input to a predetermined generator but does not control which visual operation is executed. At L2 Execution Control, it selects and invokes actual generation, editing, rendering, or other content-modifying operations. At L3 Outcome-Adaptive Control, it observes an intermediate outcome and uses that observation to change a subsequent operation within the current task. At L4 Experience-Adaptive Control, it retains experience from completed tasks and uses that experience to change decisions on future tasks. L0 Fixed Support separately denotes generators, editors, evaluators, reward models, benchmarks, and fixed pipelines without a deployed controller that makes generation-level decisions. These levels describe a progressively broader decision-making scope rather than model size, system complexity, output quality, tool or role count, or training method. Applying this framework across image, video, editing, 3D, world, slide, and user-interface generation reveals how controller capabilities have evolved and how their mechanisms are distributed across levels.

</details>


### [122] [Unified Multi-Layer Subspace Modeling for Cross-Domain OOD Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06785)
*Gerhard Krumpl,Henning Avenhaus,Horst Possegger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PRISM是一种模型无关的后处理OOD检测方法，通过将中间层和深层特征融合为统一的分层嵌入，并建模ID主子空间，结合投影子空间中的类条件马氏距离与正交残差能量两个互补信号进行OOD检测，在多种跨域基准上以单一默认配置实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后处理OOD检测方法大多依赖单一表示层（如logits或倒数第二层特征），或通过深度选择/OOD校准加权组合多层。但由于OOD分布偏移类型多样，最具信息量的表示层会因OOD类型和域的不同而剧烈变化，导致固定层选择和OOD校准聚合方式脆弱、泛化能力差。

Method: PRISM首先将中间层和深层特征融合为单一的分层嵌入向量；然后估计ID数据的主子空间；最后结合两个互补信号进行检测：(i) 在投影子空间中计算类条件马氏距离，捕捉子空间内的语义偏差；(ii) 计算正交于学习流形的残差能量，捕捉子空间外的异常。该方法避免了面向OOD调优的层权重，采用模型无关的后处理方式。

Result: 在涵盖自然图像、医学影像和工业视觉检测等多个基准上，PRISM以单一默认配置在所有评估域和架构上实现了一致的跨域SOTA OOD检测性能，且仅引入极小的推理开销。

Conclusion: PRISM通过统一建模多层特征表示而非独立聚合各层得分，有效应对了OOD多样性带来的表示层选择困难问题，在保持性能领先的同时实现了实际部署的可行性。

Abstract: Out-of-Distribution (OOD) detection remains a fundamental challenge for neural networks, whose predictions can be overconfident on inputs that deviate from the training distribution. Most post-hoc OOD detection methods derive scores from a single representation level (eg., logits or penultimate features) or combine multiple layers via depth selection or OOD-calibrated weighting. However, because OOD shifts are diverse, the most informative representation level can vary strongly across OOD types and domains, making fixed-layer choices and OOD-calibrated aggregation brittle. In this paper, we propose PRISM (Projected Representation with Intermediate-layer Subspace Modeling), a model-agnostic post-hoc OOD detection method that models a unified multi-layer feature representation rather than aggregating independently scored layers. PRISM fuses intermediate and deep features into a single hierarchical embedding, estimates an in-distribution (ID) principal subspace, and then combines two complementary signals: (i) a class-conditional Mahalanobis distance in the projected subspace and (ii) the residual energy orthogonal to the learned manifold. This simple design avoids OOD-tuned layer weighting while capturing both in-subspace semantic deviations and off-subspace anomalies. Across diverse benchmarks spanning natural images, medical imaging, and industrial visual inspection, PRISM achieves consistent state-of-the-art cross-domain OOD detection performance with a single default configuration across all evaluated domains and architectures. We further show that PRISM incurs minimal inference overhead, making it practical for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [123] [When Speech Meets Lips: Interpretable Audio-Visual Synchronization for L2 Pronunciation Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06788)
*Bowen Yu,Mingyu Huang,Yishen Liu,Yue Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个可解释的音频-视觉同步框架，用于自动发音评估（APA），通过显式建模语音-唇部时间对齐来提供可操作的二语发音训练反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有APA系统主要依赖声学信号，忽略语音与发音器官运动之间的时间同步信息，无法为发音训练提供关于时间错位的诊断性反馈。

Method: 提出可解释的音频-视觉同步框架，包含特征编码、交叉注意力融合、时延估计、稳定性量化和可视化五个模块；引入帧级时延轨迹和Lag Stability Index (LSI)来量化同步鲁棒性；并通过访谈30名参与者（10名教师和20名来自不同母语背景的学生）评估框架有效性。

Result: 通过将隐式对齐转换为可解释的表示，该框架将自动评分与可操作的计算机辅助发音训练反馈连接起来。论文提供了数据集和补充材料。

Conclusion: 该框架显式建模语音与发音运动的时间同步关系，为L2发音训练提供更丰富的诊断反馈，弥补了现有APA系统仅依赖声学信号的局限。

Abstract: Automatic Pronunciation Assessment (APA) systems have achieved strong performance with transformer-based models and self-supervised speech representations. However, most methods rely only on acoustic signals and overlook temporal synchronization between speech and articulatory movements, limiting diagnostic feedback on timing mismatches important for L2 pronunciation training. We propose an interpretable audio-visual synchronization framework that explicitly models speech-lip temporal alignment through feature encoding, cross-attention fusion, lag estimation, stability quantification, and visualization. The framework introduces frame-level lag trajectories and a Lag Stability Index (LSI) to quantify synchronization robustness. We also interviewed 30 participants, including 10 instructors and 20 students with diverse first-language backgrounds, to assess its effectiveness. By transforming implicit alignment into interpretable representations, the framework connects automatic scoring with actionable Computer-Aided Pronunciation Training feedback. Datasets and supplemental materials are available at https://www.robots.ox.ac.uk/~vgg/data/lip_reading/.

</details>


### [124] [Comparative Study of Anatomical and Learned Features in AI Models for Structural Brain MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06807)
*Boyang Yu,Miquel Lopez Escoriza,Long Chen,Arjun V. Masurkar,Narges Razavian,Carlos Fernandez-Granda*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了AI神经影像建模中的三种特征提取范式（解剖特征、CNN监督学习、ViT自监督预训练），基于18个公开数据集约8万名参与者的7项临床任务，发现线性解剖模型在诊断性能上与复杂深度学习模型相当。作者进一步提出解剖分割预训练（ASP）方法，在基础模型预训练中融入解剖信息，在生物年龄估计任务上优于现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经影像AI建模中存在多种特征提取范式（传统解剖学特征、CNN、Transformer基础模型），但缺乏对它们在临床诊断任务中性能的系统比较。研究者希望明确何种特征提取方式对于神经影像建模最为有效与可解释。

Method: 实验层面：在18个公开数据集（约80,000名参与者的3D T1加权MRI）上，对7项临床诊断任务比较三种范式：（1）解剖表面/体积计算后的线性模型，（2）CNN监督学习，（3）ViT基础模型无监督预训练+监督微调。方法层面：提出ASP（Anatomy Segmentation Pretraining）方法，将解剖信息以分割预训练任务形式注入基础模型预训练阶段。

Result: 1）基于解剖特征的线性模型在诊断性能上可匹敌复杂深度学习模型。2）CNN和预训练ViT能隐式学习相关解剖信息，无需显式特征提取。3）ASP方法在生物年龄估计任务上超越现有模型。

Conclusion: 显式解剖特征提取的简单线性模型在临床诊断中是高效且不可忽视的基线；同时，将解剖先验注入基础模型预训练（ASP）可以有效提升性能，为神经影像基础模型提供了新的改进方向。

Abstract: In this work, we comprehensively evaluate three popular feature-extraction paradigms in AI-based neuroimaging modeling: (1) computation of anatomical surfaces and volumes, (2) supervised learning with convolutional neural networks (CNNs), and (3) unsupervised pretraining of vision transformer (ViT) foundation models, followed by supervised finetuning. Our study is based on 18 publicly available datasets containing 3D structural T1-weighted MRI scans from approximately 80,000 participants across seven distinct clinical tasks. We observe that a linear model based on anatomical features matches the diagnostic performance of complex nonlinear features learned by sophisticated AI frameworks, including foundation models trained on thousands of scans. Conversely, CNNs and pretrained ViTs learn features that implicitly capture relevant anatomical information, bypassing the need for explicit feature extraction. Building upon these insights, we propose Anatomy Segmentation Pretraining (ASP), a novel method to incorporate anatomical information during foundation-model pretraining, which outperforms existing models in biological age estimation.

</details>


### [125] [Disparity Has a Sign: Stereo Matching Beyond the Zero-Disparity Plane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06809)
*Jian Shi,Xinge Yang,Chaoyang Wang,Wolfgang Heidrich,Peter Wonka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 现代立体匹配模型在视差跨越零值时性能崩溃（EPE增长4.6-37倍），因为从数据集到架构到评估协议都继承了非负几何假设（平行相机将ZDP置于无穷远，所有有限深度均为正视差）。作者提出ZDPShift基准：215个来自7部开源电影、每帧在5个ZDP位置渲染、共21,495个带稠密有符号GT的立体对。实验表明六个SOTA图像和视频立体匹配模型在ZDP移动后全部崩溃（FoundationStereo从2.24px升至75.33px EPE）。但根本解决无需新数据或参数：用SceneFlow合成监督微调训练，误差在符号区间保持平坦；仅训练解码器、冻结预训练匹配特征，均表现可比（EPE抖动0.2px内），说明预训练特征已延伸至从未训练过的负视差区域，只是输出约定丢弃了它。正值区间精度（KITTI、Middlebury、ETH3D、Sintel）基本保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 立体内容（从影院3D到VR）经常包含零视差平面后方的物体（负视差），但现代立体匹配模型在该区域系统性失败。作者指出这是一个"盲点"问题：传统校正平行相机将ZDP数学上置于无穷远，导致所有有限深度视差d=fB/z >= 0，整个标准流水线（数据集、架构、评估协议）都无法产生或测量负视差。这需要在可控条件下定量测量并揭示问题根源，而现有基准无法做到。

Method: 作者针对OSF提出三个问题：(1)评估模型的特征预测能力；(2)对预测置信度进行校准并输出校准的不确定性；(3)实施领域自适应分割。特征预测解码器将预测分割掩码作为先验注入解码层；置信度模块通过温度缩放和保序回归校准不确定性。在Prostate、BRATS2018、ISIC2018和GLiRON四个医学数据集上对STU-Net、UTNet、Swin-UNETR等六种代表性骨干进行验证。

Result: 六个SOTA模型在ZDP移动后全部崩溃：FoundationStereo在同一场景内容下EPE从2.24px升至75.33px，每个骨干都有约一半像素的视差误差超过3像素。但用SceneFlow合成的有符号监督微调（不增加新数据或参数）后误差在整个符号范围内保持平坦；且仅训练解码器、冻结预训练匹配特征时六个骨干均表现可比（EPE在0.2px内抖动）。正值区间在KITTI、Middlebury、ETH3D、Sintel上的精度基本保持。

Conclusion: 预训练匹配特征实际上已经具备处理负视差的能力——它们从未被训练用于负视差区域，但特征本身已延伸至该范围；真正丢弃这一能力的是"输出约定"（即解码器/输出头只输出非负视差）。因此问题的修复不需要新数据或新架构，只需在训练/微调中让输出头覆盖有符号的视差范围，即可在保持正值区间精度的同时解决负视差崩溃问题。

Abstract: Modern stereo matching models fail when disparity crosses zero, with end-point error (EPE) rising by 4.6-37$\times$. Yet stereoscopic content, from cinema 3D to VR, routinely contains objects behind the zero-disparity plane (ZDP), corresponding to negative disparities. The blind spot cascades through datasets, architectures, and evaluation protocols, all of which inherit the non-negative geometry. Rectified parallel cameras place ZDP at infinity, so every finite depth yields $d=fB/z \ge 0$ by construction, and nothing within the standard pipeline can violate, or even measure, a negative disparity. To measure it, we propose \textit{ZDPShift}, a benchmark of $21{,}495$ stereo pairs from seven cinematographer-authored open movies, each frame rendered at five zero-disparity-plane positions with dense signed ground truth. Six state-of-the-art image and video stereo matching models collapse once the plane moves. On identical scene content, FoundationStereo goes from $2.24$ px EPE to $75.33$px, with every backbone leaving roughly half of all pixels exceeding a three-pixel disparity error. What is missing, however, is not the underlying matching capability. % The capability itself, however, is already present. Training on supervision synthesized from SceneFlow, which adds no new data or parameters, keeps the error flat across the signed range. Training only the decoder, with the pretrained matching features frozen, performs comparably across all six backbones, with EPE jittering within $0.2$px. Thus, the pretrained features already extend to the negative regime they were never trained on, and only the output convention discarded it. Meanwhile, positive-regime accuracy on KITTI, Middlebury, ETH3D, and Sintel is largely preserved.

</details>


### [126] [Generalist Open-World Temporal Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06823)
*Cristian Sminchisescu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出通用开放世界时间感知架构（GOWTPA），主张下一代AI系统应原生支持时间与多模态输入输出。该架构将识别、结构化预测和仿真视为共享生成基座上的不同条件化操作，使感知与合成在同一表征层上统一，作为更广泛多模态智能与物理AI的潜在基础层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代AI系统需要在共享世界表征中整合对话、感知、预测、推理与合成，但目前大尺度生成式视频预训练虽涌现出类跨模态与推理能力，却多通过语言探针访问或以逼真视频表达，显式的语义、几何与时序结构未被充分暴露。需要一个能明确表达结构化世界状态、维持身份一致性并处理未知项的通用时间感知系统。

Method: 提出GOWTPA架构范式：将感知与合成视为同一共享生成基座上的不同条件化操作——识别、结构化预测与仿真均从统一生成底层中衍生，推理与具身策略建立在该生成的世界状态之上。模型从原始多模态流中推断几何、关节点、语义、交互结构与不确定性，维持跨遮挡和视角变化的身份一致性，并能在遇到未知时弃权或扩展本体。

Result: 论文为概念框架/立场文章（position paper），未给出实验性结果，而是基于近期大规模生成式视频预训练中跨模态与推理能力涌现的观察，论证统一感知-合成架构的可行性，并给出架构性论述作为研究议程的蓝图。

Conclusion: 通用时间感知应作为多层次多模态智能与物理AI的基础层。通过共享生成式基底实现识别、预测与仿真的条件化统一，可同时支持理解、预测、反事实推理与可控合成，为构建连贯的、时间持久的开放世界感知系统指明方向。

Abstract: The next generation of artificial intelligence systems will likely be natively temporal and multimodal in both inputs and outputs: able to converse, perceive, predict, reason, and synthesize through a shared world representation. Realizing this requires a temporal perceptual substrate integrating sensory streams, language, and structured outputs within a multimodal world model. We seek a generalist open-world perceptual system that represents biological forms, natural physical structures, and artifacts, and their interactions, as a coherent, temporally persistent process. The model should infer geometry, articulation, semantics, interaction structure, and uncertainty from raw multimodal streams; maintain identity through occlusion and viewpoint change; generalize across species, forms, mechanisms, and materials; and abstain or expand its ontology when encountering the unknown. The objective is a structured world state supporting understanding, prediction, counterfactual reasoning, and controllable synthesis. Recent work suggests that some cross-modal and reasoning-like capabilities can emerge from large-scale generative video pretraining, reminiscent of language-model scaling. Yet these capabilities are often accessed through language probes or expressed through photorealistic video, leaving explicit semantic, geometric, or temporal structure largely unexposed. This paper articulates an alternative and complementary paradigm: perception and synthesis as distinct conditionings within a shared Generalist Open-World Temporal Perception Architecture (GOWTPA). Recognition, structured prediction, and simulation arise as different conditionings of the same generative substrate, while reasoning and embodiment-specific policies build upon the resulting world state. This positions generalist temporal perception as a potential foundation layer for broader multimodal intelligence and physical AI.

</details>


### [127] [A Shared-Backbone Approach for Multi-Task MedMNIST Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06838)
*Stefan-Dorian Gavril,Andrei Arhire,Adrian Iftene*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了11个异构MedMNIST数据集的医学图像多任务分类问题，发现并解决了数据分辨率域偏移问题，通过优化架构正则化，最终以ConvNeXt-Tiny骨干网络配合标签平滑在竞赛中取得第六名（harmonic-mean macro-F1为0.73294）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务生物医学分类要求模型能够跨不同模态和类别分布进行泛化，这是一个具有挑战性的实际问题。作者旨在探索在11个异构MedMNIST数据集上，通过共享骨干网络实现多任务分类的有效方案。

Method: 采用三个不同的骨干网络（backbone），各配备任务专属线性头（task-specific linear heads），以各任务macro-F1的调和平均数（harmonic mean）作为评估指标。研究中发现MedMNIST API与评估环境之间存在分辨率域偏移（resolution domain shift），通过消除该不一致性并针对具体架构优化正则化策略来提升性能。

Result: 最优配置为ConvNeXt-Tiny骨干网络结合标签平滑（label smoothing），在Tensor Reloaded: Multi-Task MedMNIST竞赛中取得harmonic-mean macro-F1为0.73294的成绩，在官方竞赛阶段结束时排名第六。

Conclusion: 解决数据预处理中的域偏移问题以及针对骨干网络定制正则化方法，是多任务生物医学分类性能提升的关键因素；该共享骨干网络方案是有效的，代码已公开。

Abstract: Multi-task biomedical classification requires models to generalize across disparate modalities and class distributions. We study 11 heterogeneous MedMNIST datasets using the harmonic mean of per-task macro-F1. We evaluate three backbones with task-specific linear heads. We identify a resolution domain shift between the MedMNIST API and evaluation environment. Resolving this inconsistency and optimizing architecture-specific regularization substantially improved performance. Our best configuration, a ConvNeXt-Tiny backbone with label smoothing, achieved a leaderboard harmonic-mean macro-F1 of 0.73294 in the Tensor Reloaded: Multi-Task MedMNIST competition, ranking sixth at the close of the official competition phase. Our implementation is publicly available at: https://github.com/GavrilStefan-Dorian/A-Shared-Backbone-Approach-for-Multi-Task-MedMNIST-Classification

</details>


### [128] [RAIDAL: Redundancy-Aware Information Density Active Learning for CTC-Based Continuous Sign Language Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06843)
*Rafael A. Diniz Augusto,Gabriel L. Oliveira,Erickson R. Nascimento*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RAIDAL（冗余感知信息密度主动学习）方法，利用连续手语识别（CSLR）模型中CTC解码器的对齐峰来限制主动学习评分仅关注由解码器对齐的gloss区域，避免视频中时间冗余对样本选择的影响，从而在低标注预算下实现更强的数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续手语识别（CSLR）的发展受限于连续视频流标注成本高昂；主动学习虽有潜力降低标注成本，但标准采集函数不适用于弱对齐的手语视频——这些视频中手语动作与静止姿态、不规则停顿、类手语动作及时间冗余帧交织，导致采集分数被与解码gloss序列无关的时间步所干扰，扭曲视频的信息量估计。

Method: 现代CSLR模型中已内嵌CTC解码器，其对齐峰能指示模型在特征序列中定位每个预测gloss的位置，为零额外标注成本的主动学习采集函数提供时间结构。由此提出RAIDAL（Redundancy-Aware Information Density Active Learning），复用CTC解码器将基于表示（representation-based）的评分限制在解码器对齐的gloss区域，而非让采集函数暴露于整个未过滤的视频。

Result: 在三个数据集和两种架构上，RAIDAL在大词汇量、预算受限场景下相比竞争基线取得了最强的数据效率提升，在小词汇量大预算场景下也保持竞争力。

Conclusion: RAIDAL通过利用CTC解码器已有的对齐信息作为时间结构来源，在不增加标注成本的前提下显著提升了CSLR主动学习在低预算场景下的数据效率，代码已公开。

Abstract: Continuous sign language recognition (CSLR) is a key technology for accessibility, yet its development remains limited by the high cost of annotating continuous video streams. Active learning offers a path toward mitigating this cost, but standard acquisition functions are not designed for weakly aligned sign language videos, where sign executions are interleaved with rest poses, irregular pauses, sign-like motion, and temporally redundant frames. This temporal redundancy can undermine sample selection, as acquisition scores may be influenced by timesteps from regions that are not associated with the decoded gloss sequence, distorting the video's estimated informativeness. In this work, we show that modern CSLR models already contain a mechanism for identifying gloss-level temporal evidence: the CTC decoder. Although typically used only during inference, its alignment peaks indicate where the model localizes each predicted gloss in the feature sequence, providing a source of temporal structure for active learning acquisition functions at zero additional labeling cost. Thus, we introduce RAIDAL (Redundancy-Aware Information Density Active Learning), which repurposes the CTC decoder to restrict representation-based scoring to decoder-aligned gloss regions, rather than exposing the acquisition function to the entire unfiltered video. Across three datasets and two architectures, RAIDAL achieves its strongest data-efficiency gains over competing baselines in large-vocabulary, budget-limited settings, while remaining competitive in the smaller-vocabulary, large-budget setting. The code used in this work is publicly available at github.com/verlab/RAIDAL.

</details>


### [129] [Organization of Valence and Arousal in Vision-Language Representations of Built Environments: Insights from the EMOIS Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06870)
*Madoka Yonekura,Katsunori Kohda,Nobuhiko Muramoto,Takahiro Yamaguchi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了EMOIS数据集，包含1,544张真实建成环境图像及日本人参与者对每张图像约120条的效价和唤醒度评分。利用CLIP表征进行预测性和几何分析，发现效价比唤醒度在表征空间中具有更强、更一致的组织结构；与OASIS跨数据集比较揭示了情感组织的差异；回归分析在EMOIS内部实现了效价0.865和唤醒度0.807的平均R²。该工作为建成环境的情感计算研究提供了密集标注资源和系统分析方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建成环境的视觉感知会影响人们在日常生活中的情感印象，但这些印象如何在视觉基础模型（如CLIP）中被表征仍缺乏系统研究。为此需要一个专门的、具有密集情感标注的真实建成环境图像数据集来支撑这一领域的系统化探索。

Method: 1) 构建EMOIS数据集，包含1,544张真实建成环境图像，通过大规模网络调查从日本成年人收集每张图像约120条图像诱发效价和唤醒度评分。2) 使用CLIP表征对效价和唤醒度进行预测性分析和几何分析（探讨两类情感维度在表征空间中的编码方式与组织方式）。3) 与OASIS通用情感图像基准进行跨数据集对比。4) 采用重复内部留出评估进行回归分析。5) 构建基于示例的界面以支持预测情感值的定性解释。

Result: 效价在表征空间中表现出比唤醒度更强且更一致的组织结构；跨数据集分析发现EMOIS与OASIS在情感组织上存在差异；回归分析在EMOIS内部取得高预测性能，效价平均R²为0.865，唤醒度平均R²为0.807。

Conclusion: EMOIS作为密集标注的建成环境情感数据集，为理解建成环境在视觉基础模型中的情感表征提供了系统性证据，揭示了效价和唤醒度在CLIP空间中的差异化编码特性，并可作为未来建成环境情感计算研究的基础资源。

Abstract: Visual perception of built environments contributes to the affective impressions that people form in everyday life. However, how these impressions are represented within vision foundation models remains largely unexplored. To support the systematic investigation of this subject, we introduce the Emotional Impression of Spaces (EMOIS) dataset, comprising 1,544 real-world built-environment images. Each image is annotated with image-evoked valence and arousal ratings collected from Japanese adults by conducting a large-scale web-based survey, with approximately 120 ratings per image. Using Contrastive Language--Image Pre-training (CLIP) representations, we perform predictive and geometric analyses to systematically investigate how valence and arousal are encoded and organized within the representation space. These analyses reveal that valence exhibited stronger and more coherent organization than arousal. Cross-dataset analyses with the Open Affective Standardized Image Set (OASIS), a benchmark dataset of general affective photographs, reveal differences in affective organization between the two datasets. Regression analyses demonstrate high predictive performance for valence and arousal within EMOIS, with mean coefficients of determination of 0.865 and 0.807, respectively, across repeated internal hold-out evaluations. Finally, we present an example-based interface illustrating how learned representations can support qualitative interpretation of predicted affective values. These findings can help elucidate affective representations of built environments and establish EMOIS as a densely annotated resource for future affective computing research in this domain.

</details>


### [130] [Novel Methods for Catheter and Guidewire Segmentation in X-ray Fluoroscopy under a Federated Learning Setting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06876)
*Chayun Kongtongvattana*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种面向导管和导丝分析的结构感知联邦学习框架，包含四个核心贡献：CathAction基准数据集、形状敏感损失函数、联邦学习中的投影梯度下降对抗优化，以及结构感知扩散模型用于视频合成，旨在不集中患者数据的情况下实现跨机构协同训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 血管内介入手术依赖X射线透视引导下对导管和导丝进行实时操作，准确的视觉分析对手术安全至关重要。然而，基于学习的方法受限于结构复杂性、数据稀缺性，以及隐私法规禁止跨机构集中式训练的问题。

Method: 1) 构建CathAction基准数据集，包含超过60万标注帧和4万分割掩膜；2) 设计形状敏感损失函数，将掩膜转化为符号距离图并在结构特征空间中进行比较；3) 将该损失扩展到联邦学习框架中，保持异质客户端数据下的几何一致性；4) 在联邦学习中引入投影梯度下降和对抗优化；5) 提出结构感知扩散框架，结合结构监督与域自适应重建目标合成导管和导丝视频序列。

Result: 形状敏感损失在五个骨干网络上将Dice系数提升最多2.9个点；在联邦学习中，当客户端从4个扩展到8个时，mIoU比联邦平均高出最多3个点；投影梯度下降方法在真实动物数据上将mIoU提升超过10个点；结构感知扩散框架降低Frechet视频距离并保持视觉保真度；将合成序列纳入联邦训练在数据稀缺条件下将Dice分数从44%提升到51%，在四个保留测试站点均有增益。

Conclusion: 这些贡献推进了隐私保护的导管和导丝分析，支持无需集中患者数据或大量人工标注的协作式训练。

Abstract: Endovascular procedures rely on real-time manipulation of thin instruments, catheters and guidewires, under X-ray fluoroscopy guidance, where accurate visual analysis is essential for procedural safety. Learning-based methods are constrained by structural complexity, data scarcity, and privacy regulations precluding centralised training across institutions. This thesis presents a structure-aware federated learning framework for catheter and guidewire analysis, with four contributions evaluated on real-animal and phantom data.
  A benchmark dataset, CathAction, is introduced for catheterisation analysis, with over 600,000 annotated frames and 40,000 segmentation masks. A shape-sensitive loss transforms masks into signed distance maps compared in a structural feature space, improving Dice coefficient by up to 2.9 points across five backbones.
  This is extended to federated learning with shape-sensitive loss, preserving geometric consistency under heterogeneous client data and outperforming federated averaging by up to three points in mean intersection-over-union as clients scale from four to eight. Federated learning with projected gradient descent adds adversarial optimisation, raising mean intersection-over-union by over ten points on real-animal data.
  Finally, a structure-aware diffusion framework synthesises catheter and guidewire video sequences, combining structural supervision with a domain-adaptive reconstruction objective, reducing Frechet video distance over a strong baseline while maintaining visual fidelity. Incorporating synthetic sequences into federated training raises the Dice score from 44 to 51 percent under data scarcity, with gains across four held-out sites.
  Together, these contributions advance privacy-preserving catheter and guidewire analysis, supporting collaborative training without centralising patient data or large amounts of manual annotation.

</details>


### [131] [Contextual Observer Grounding: Evaluating Situated Spatial Reasoning in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06880)
*Mimo Shirasaka,Haochen Zhang,Yonatan Bisk*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"上下文观察者接地"（Contextual Observer Grounding）这一能力概念，指视觉语言模型（VLM）能否通过环境知识、活动背景与常识推断说话者的视角，并从该视角理解情境化空间关系。据此构建了POVBench基准数据集，包含3D场景与自然语言查询，区分推断式（Inferred）、明确陈述式（Stated）和给定式（Given）三种观察者接地形式。实验发现，最先进的VLM在基于方向性语言定位目标时仍具挑战，即使观察者接地被显式给出也表现不佳；而将观察者相对空间推理显式分解则能显著改善目标定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具身任务（如机器人）中，理解空间关系需要从说话者的情境化视角出发。人类可以从共享环境知识、活动上下文和常识推断说话者视角，但现有VLM虽表现出一定空间推理能力，其能否从上下文线索推断说话者视角并据此解读情境化空间关系尚未明确。作者希望系统性地探究这一关键但被忽视的能力。

Method: 作者首先定义"上下文观察者接地"能力并分为三种形式：推断式（Inferred）、明确陈述式（Stated）和给定式（Given）观察者接地。构建POVBench数据集，包含3D场景和多视角观测，配合自然语言句子，要求模型从情境化空间与上下文线索中定位未见或欠指定的目标对象。通过解耦不同接地形式来评估各类最先进VLM的空间定位能力。

Result: 实验表明：现有最先进的VLM在根据方向性语言定位目标时仍面临重大挑战，即使在观察者接地被显式明确的情况下，模型准确率依然偏低；相反，当将观察者相对空间推理进行显式分解与拆解时，模型的目标定位性能得到明显提升。

Conclusion: VLM的"上下文观察者接地"能力尚不成熟，尤其在基于方向性语言进行目标定位时表现薄弱；显式的空间推理分解可以有效弥补这一不足。POVBench为评估和改进VLM在具身情境下的情境化空间理解提供了有价值的基准，未来工作可沿着更系统化的推理链与更多样化的3D场景扩展。

Abstract: Reasoning over language instructions in embodied tasks such as robotics often requires understanding spatial relations from a speaker's situated perspective. Humans infer such perspectives from shared environmental knowledge, activity context, and commonsense. Recent vision-language models (VLMs) appear capable of spatial reasoning, but their ability to infer a speaker's viewpoint from contextual cues and interpret situated spatial relations from that viewpoint remains unclear. We call this capability contextual observer grounding. To study this capability, we construct the Point-of-View Benchmark (POVBench), a dataset of 3D scenes and queries that disentangles Inferred, Stated, and Given forms of observer grounding in natural embodied communication. Given multi-view observations and a natural-language sentence, models must localize unseen or underspecified targets from situated spatial and contextual cues. Across state-of-the-art VLMs, localizing targets from directional language remains challenging, even when observer grounding is made explicit. We find that explicit breakdowns of observer-relative spatial reasoning improve target localization. Our project page is available at https://mimo-owl.github.io/POVBench/.

</details>


### [132] [BEFORE THE FLIP: Measuring Hidden Score Shifts In Quantized Vision Language Models Before The Answer Changes for Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06922)
*Sourajit Saha,Shubhashis Roy Dipta,Shaswati Saha,Nobin Sarwar,Yuxuan Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 BEFORE THE FLIP 方法，用于测量 VLM 低比特量化压缩后潜在得分（而非最终答案）的变化。通过比较压缩引起的得分变化与替换图像 token 引起的得分变化，发现 4-bit 压缩比 8-bit 压缩更显著地改变了 yes/no 得分的差距，尽管只有极少数答案实际改变。同时研究表明按问题逐组调整精度并未带来超过随机对照的可靠收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化压缩虽然不改变 VLM 的最终答案，但可能隐藏了底层 softmax 得分/对数概率的变化（例如 yes 与 no 的得分差距缩小）。现有评估只关注最终回答是否变化，忽略了这些隐藏的得分偏移，无法充分衡量量化对模型内部行为的影响。
